In Java 9+, the tools.jar was removed from the JDK filesystem\n2. It's now part of the jdk.compiler module\n3. Maven still tries to reference the old path at `$JAVA_HOME/lib/tools.jar`\n\n### Solution Approaches\n\n#### Approach 1: Remove Unnecessary tools Dependency (Recommended)\nIf the project doesn't actually use the tools.jar API, remove or update the dependency:\n- Most modern Java projects don't need explicit tools.jar dependency\n- The compiler is accessed through standard APIs\n\n#### Approach 2: Add System Scope Dependency\nIf the dependency is necessary:\n```xml\n<dependency>\n  <groupId>com.sun</groupId>\n  <artifactId>tools</artifactId>\n  <version>1.8.0</version>\n  <scope>system</scope>\n  <systemPath>${java.home}/../lib/tools.jar</systemPath>\n</dependency>\n```\n\n#### Approach 3: Use Maven Compiler Plugin with Fork\nConfigure Maven compiler to use system Java compiler:\n```xml\n<plugin>\n  <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>\n  <artifactId>maven-compiler-plugin</artifactId>\n  <configuration>\n    <fork>true</fork>\n    <executable>${java.home}/bin/javac</executable>\n  </configuration>\n</plugin>\n```\n\n## Checking Project Dependencies\n\nTo see all dependencies and their resolution status:\n```bash\nmvn dependency:tree\nmvn dependency:resolve\n```\n\n## Testing the Fix\n\nAfter applying fix:\n```bash\nmvn clean compile\nmvn clean test\nmvn clean package\n```\n\n## References\n- Maven Compiler Plugin Configuration: https://maven.apache.org/plugins/maven-compiler-plugin/\n- Java Module System and tools: https://docs.oracle.com/javase/9/migrate/\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":243,"cache_read_input_tokens":38313,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":243,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d765f11d-47d7-4138-9295-a7c7d3171530","uuid":"e93eeedd-b2ae-4bd4-8a3b-58fc64d912f3"}

In Java 9+, the tools.jar was removed from the JDK filesystem\n2. It's now part of the jdk.compiler module\n3. Maven still tries to reference the old path at `$JAVA_HOME/lib/tools.jar`\n\n### Solution Approaches\n\n#### Approach 1: Remove Unnecessary tools Dependency (Recommended)\nIf the project doesn't actually use the tools.jar API, remove or update the dependency:\n- Most modern Java projects don't need explicit tools.jar dependency\n- The compiler is accessed through standard APIs\n\n#### Approach 2: Add System Scope Dependency\nIf the dependency is necessary:\n```xml\n<dependency>\n  <groupId>com.sun</groupId>\n  <artifactId>tools</artifactId>\n  <version>1.8.0</version>\n  <scope>system</scope>\n  <systemPath>${java.home}/../lib/tools.jar</systemPath>\n</dependency>\n```\n\n#### Approach 3: Use Maven Compiler Plugin with Fork\nConfigure Maven compiler to use system Java compiler:\n```xml\n<plugin>\n  <groupId>org.apache.maven.plugins</groupId>\n  <artifactId>maven-compiler-plugin</artifactId>\n  <configuration>\n    <fork>true</fork>\n    <executable>${java.home}/bin/javac</executable>\n  </configuration>\n</plugin>\n```\n\n## Checking Project Dependencies\n\nTo see all dependencies and their resolution status:\n```bash\nmvn dependency:tree\nmvn dependency:resolve\n```\n\n## Testing the Fix\n\nAfter applying fix:\n```bash\nmvn clean compile\nmvn clean test\nmvn clean package\n```\n\n## References\n- Maven Compiler Plugin Configuration: https://maven.apache.org/plugins/maven-compiler-plugin/\n- Java Module System and tools: https://docs.oracle.com/javase/9/migrate/\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

If parent defines a problematic dependency, it affects all children.\n\n## Working with Inherited Dependencies\n\n### Find all inherited dependencies:\n```bash\nmvn dependency:tree -DoutputFile=dependency-tree.txt\n```\n\n### Exclude a transitive dependency:\n```xml\n<dependency>\n  <groupId>some.group</groupId>\n  <artifactId>some-artifact</artifactId>\n  <exclusions>\n    <exclusion>\n      <groupId>problematic.group</groupId>\n      <artifactId>problematic-artifact</artifactId>\n    </exclusion>\n  </exclusions>\n</dependency>\n```\n\n### Override parent dependency version:\n```xml\n<dependencyManagement>\n  <dependencies>\n    <dependency>\n      <groupId>group.id</groupId>\n      <artifactId>artifact-id</artifactId>\n      <version>new.version</version>\n    </dependency>\n  </dependencies>\n</dependencyManagement>\n```\n\n## Debugging Dependency Resolution\n\n### View full dependency resolution:\n```bash\nmvn -X dependency:tree 2>&1 | grep -A5 \"tools\"\n```\n\n### Check if dependency comes from parent:\n```bash\nmvn help:describe -Dplugin=org.apache.maven.plugins:maven-compiler-plugin\n```\n\n## Common Maven Properties\n\nStandard properties in pom.xml:\n- `${java.home}` - JDK home directory\n- `${project.build.directory}` - Target directory\n- `${basedir}` - Project base directory\n- `${user.home}` - User's home directory\n\n## References\n- Maven POM Reference: https://maven.apache.org/pom.html\n- Dependency Management: https://maven.apache.org/guides/introduction/introduction-to-dependency-mechanism.html\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":638,"cache_read_input_tokens":38556,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":638,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d765f11d-47d7-4138-9295-a7c7d3171530","uuid":"d8d72c4e-adeb-48c1-8fd0-801de74c0cf2"}
